备注：Scheme 2's standard 3a row (77% isolated yield) is the same 0.1/0.2 mmol, Pd(PPh3)4/DPEphos/Yb(OTf)3/Cs2CO3/THF, 55 °C, 8 h experiment as Table 1 entry 15 and is merged as cross-page evidence rather than duplicated.

备注：The page-7 general procedure for [3+2] products supplies the ordered 30 min catalyst pre-stirring, substrate/additive charge under argon, 8 h heated hold, celite filtration, vacuum concentration, and silica-gel purification. It is merged into this existing experiment rather than represented as a duplicate reaction.

备注：The page-7 [3+2] general procedure prints Pd(PPh3)4 as 0.005 mmol/5 mol% and DPEphos as 0.007 mmol/7.5 mol% on a 0.1 mmol substrate scale. Both printed absolute amounts and loadings are retained; the rounded 0.007 mmol ligand charge is not silently recalculated.

备注：The page-7 general procedure for [4+2] products supplies the ordered 30 min catalyst pre-stirring, substrate/additive charge under argon, 8 h heated hold, celite filtration, vacuum concentration, and silica-gel purification. It is merged into this existing experiment rather than represented as a duplicate reaction.

备注：The page-7 [4+2] general procedure prints Pd2dba3 as 0.002 mmol/2.5 mol% and (R)-Ph-PHOX as 0.007 mmol/7.5 mol% on a 0.1 mmol substrate scale. Both printed absolute amounts and loadings are retained; the rounded mmol charges are not silently recalculated.
